Furthermore, for systems with conservation laws, we extend it to Conservation-constrained Bi-CFM (CBi-CFM). Across the classic Lorenz, Circuit, and high-dimensional Lorenz 96 systems, Bi-CFM improves five distribution-level metrics over baselines while achieving a speedup of more than two orders of magnitude. In the three-body planet-planet scattering problem in planetary dynamics, CBi-CFM better respects conservation laws, with conservation errors comparable to those of the ground truth. Finally, on real observations of globular clusters, collisional million-body systems shaped by $\sim 10^{10}$ years (10 Gyr) of evolution, our method represents an advance in accuracy, establishing a scalable route to solving inverse problems of long-timescale real-world chaotic dynamics.

</details>


### [249] [Difference-Making without Making a Difference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24832)
*Sander Beckers*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文批判 Andreas 与 Günther 关于实际因果关系的七个定义，指出其最新的“事实性差异制造”定义同时实例化了三类账户，因此三类划分并不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在检验 Andreas 与 Günther 对实际因果关系理论的分类是否具有实质区分，并评估其最新定义与此前六个定义在关键案例中的表现。

Method: 通过概念分析与案例比较，作者考察最新定义是否只属于“事实性差异制造”类型，并将其与其他六个因果账户在若干关键例子上的判定结果进行对照。

Result: 分析表明，最新定义同时符合事实性差异制造、反事实差异制造和规律性三类账户特征；在关键案例比较中，该问题进一步削弱了全部七个账户的解释力。

Conclusion: 论文认为 Andreas 与 Günther 所提出的三分类缺乏真正差异，其七个实际因果关系定义都面临系统性问题。

Abstract: Over a series of seven papers, Andreas & Günther have introduced seven definitions of actual causation and have classified them as belonging to three different, competing, types of accounts: factual difference-making, counterfactual difference-making, and regularity-based. I show that their most recent - factual difference-making - definition instantiates all three types, thereby proving that these are distinctions without a difference. I further compare their novel account to the other six accounts on several crucial examples, revealing that this undermines all seven of their accounts.

</details>


### [250] [Accuracy and Satisfaction in Multi-Turn LLM Dialogues for NFR Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24834)
*Ali Pourghasemi Fatideh,Wilder Baldwin,Maria Dhakal,Collin McMillan,Sepideh Ghanavati*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究开发者与基于LLM的对话助手在非功能需求（NFR）评估中的多轮交互质量与准确性，发现用户常认同LLM判断但与专家真值相比准确率较低；较长回复和过多信息提供轮次降低满意度，主动式交互提升满意度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软件开发LLM评测主要关注功能正确性，忽视了非功能需求这类模糊、上下文依赖、跨代码多处关联的问题。NFR评估需要衡量多轮对话中的输出正确性、推理质量、代码定位能力及用户交互体验。

Method: 研究招募49名程序员，让其使用GitHub Copilot围绕iTrust代码库评估148个源自HIPAA合规要求的NFR，并从需求满足程度、推理、代码定位三个维度进行分析；同时建模用户满意度与对话特征之间的关系。

Result: 开发者倾向于同意LLM给出的评估，但与专家标注的ground truth相比整体准确性较低。用户满意度方面，系统回复越长、提供信息的轮次越多，满意度越低；主动式交互与更高满意度相关。

Conclusion: LLM对话助手在NFR评估中具有协作潜力，但当前准确性不足且易产生用户过度信任。未来系统应改进多轮推理、证据定位、简洁表达和主动交互设计，以更可靠地支持NFR评估。

Abstract: LLM-based dialogue assistants have become mainstream tools for software developers, yet current evaluation benchmarks focus exclusively on functional correctness. This leaves a critical gap in assessing the quality and accuracy of these conversations when handling Non-Functional Requirements (NFRs), which are inherently vague, context-dependent, and involve many parts of a program. Evaluating how well these systems support collaborative reasoning about NFRs requires methods that go beyond single-turn accuracy to capture both the correctness of the system's outputs and the quality of the multi-turn interaction. In this paper, we investigate the accuracy and quality of multi-turn conversations between developers and an LLM-based agent in the domain of Health Insurance Portability and Accountability Act (HIPAA) regulatory compliance. We hired 49 programmers to interact with GitHub Copilot to assess 148 HIPAA-derived NFRs against the iTrust codebase, a system designed to comply with HIPAA regulations, across three dimensions: requirement satisfaction level, reasoning, and code localization. We find that developers tend to agree with LLM assessments, but accuracy against expert ground truth is low. We model user satisfaction and find that longer system responses and more information-providing turns negatively affect user satisfaction, whereas proactive interactions positively affect it. Our findings provide insights for designing LLM-based dialogue systems that support NFR assessment.

</details>


### [251] [Grading the Grader: Lessons from Evaluating an Agentic Data Analysis System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24839)
*Tian Zheng,Kai-Tai Hsu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究如何可靠评估智能体式数据分析系统的复杂输出，并提出三层人机协同评分级联，以区分真实答案不一致与评分伪差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体数据分析系统会同时产生代码、数值结果和文字诊断，输出形态复杂，传统单轮LLM评测方法难以准确判断其正确性，因此需要提升自动评分的可靠性并识别评分误差来源。

Method: 作者在DSGym的153个QRData数值任务上评估多智能体数据分析系统LAMBDA，构建三层评分流程：严格正则匹配、基于LLM的宽松评分、基于片段的人类检查；同时测试关键词锚定抽取、迭代提示机制以及原问题重新注入等策略。

Result: 两个自动评分器均达到观察到的100%精确率；宽松评分器相对人工标签召回率为97%。关键词锚定抽取相比最后数字启发式使严格评分器召回率提高60个百分点。迭代提示将评分运行成功率从36%提升到97%，宽松通过率从16%提升到46%；原问题重新注入无额外收益。

Conclusion: 结合非生成式与生成式策略的分层评分级联能显著提升智能体数据分析系统评估的可靠性；评分质量关键在于答案抽取与模板提示，而变量类型是影响评分管线动态和结果等级的重要元数据因素。

Abstract: Agentic data analysis systems produce rich outputs, including code, numerical results, and verbal diagnostics. This makes them more challenging to evaluate than single-turn LLM responses. It is therefore necessary to distinguish genuine disagreement between an agent's output and a ground-truth answer from grading artifacts. We investigate how reliably automated graders assess such a system and what strategies improve grading quality by applying LAMBDA, a multi-agent data-analysis system, on 153 numerical QRData tasks from DSGym. We develop and evaluate a three-layer human-AI grading cascade: strict regex matching, LLM-based lenient grading, and snippet-based human inspection, which combines non-GenAI and GenAI strategies with different failure profiles. Both automated graders achieve 100% observed precision (0/70 false positives). The lenient grader's recall is 97% against human labels. A keyword-anchored extraction pipeline raises the strict grader's recall by 60 percentage points over a last-number heuristic; the lenient grader is architecturally parser-independent. An iterative nudge mechanism raises grading run success from 36% to 97% and lenient-pass rates from 16% to 46%; comparing nudging with and without original-question re-injection shows that re-injection offers no benefit, confirming the nudge as an answer template cue. We further observe in this case study that variable type is the task metadata field most consistently associated with grading pipeline dynamics and observed outcome grades.

</details>


### [252] [Matching Tasks to Objectives: Fine-Tuning and Prompt-Tuning Strategies for Encoder-Decoder Pre-trained Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24841)
*Ahmad Pouramini,Hesham Faili*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 MTO（Match Task to Objective）框架，用于为具体任务匹配合适的预训练/适配目标，并通过目标一致的模板与无监督适配显著提升少样本生成和问答任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示学习已成为 NLP 的重要范式，但不同预训练目标如何影响 encoder-decoder 预训练语言模型在生成、问答及常识知识补全/检索任务中的表现仍缺乏系统指导。作者希望解决任务需求、预训练目标、适配目标和微调模板之间不匹配导致的性能受限问题。

Method: 研究比较多种预训练目标对下游任务的影响，提出 MTO 框架来自动判断任务应匹配的目标；基于识别出的目标，自动构造任务相关数据进行无监督适配训练；在微调阶段设计与预训练和适配目标一致的新模板；同时将该思想扩展到 prompt-tuning，为软提示设计与优化提供指导。

Result: 当目标与任务需求对齐时，该方法在少样本设置下相较传统方法可获得超过 120% 的性能提升，显著优于相关少样本方法；在全数据场景下也超过基线。同时，该策略也显著提升了 prompt-tuning 的性能。

Conclusion: 论文表明，多目标预训练/适配以及任务-目标对齐对提示学习至关重要。MTO 为特定任务选择合适目标、构造适配数据和设计提示模板提供了系统化方法，可有效提升 encoder-decoder 模型在生成与问答任务中的性能。

Abstract: Prompt-based learning has emerged as a dominant paradigm in natural language processing. This study explores the impact of diverse pre-training objectives on the performance of encoder-decoder pre-trained language models across generation and question answering tasks, with a focus on commonsense knowledge retrieval and completion. We highlight the benefits of incorporating multiple objectives during both pre-training and fine-tuning stages. We introduce the Match Task to Objective (MTO) framework and methods for determining the appropriate objective for a given task. This framework offers automated methods to prepare task-related data for adaptation through unsupervised training, based on the identified objective. In the fine-tuning stage, we design novel templates that align with the objectives of the pre-training and adaptation stages. When aligned with task requirements, these strategies can achieve a performance gain of over 120\% compared to conventional methods in few-shot settings. They significantly outperform related works in few-shot settings and exceed the baseline even in full-dataset scenarios. Furthermore, we extend this approach to include prompt-tuning methodologies, providing guidance for more effective soft prompt engineering and optimization. Our strategies significantly enhance prompt-tuning performance as well. These insights hold substantial value, precisely guiding the selection and optimization of models customized for specific tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/puraminy/MTO/

</details>


### [253] [World Models in Pieces: Structural Certification for General Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24842)
*Yikai Lu,Yifei Wu,Xinyu Lu,Tongxin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出大世界环境中通用智能体无法在所有情形下普遍可靠，提出“结构化认证”框架，将有界的目标条件性能转化为对内部世界模型中特定转移的逐项保证，从而定位长时程规划中可靠的关键转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂大世界中，智能体能力必然具有专门化特征；传统最坏情形或统一性能保证无法区分关键瓶颈理解不足与无关失败，因此难以指导可靠部署。

Method: 作者首先形式化证明一般智能体不具备普适性，使标准最坏情况分析失去信息量；随后提出转移局部的结构化认证框架，并构造利用深度组合目标筛选特定状态转移的算法，将目标条件表现映射为内部世界模型的条目级误差保证。

Result: 对于满足这些深度组合目标的一般智能体，论文证明其结构化世界模型在被认证转移上具有 O(1/n)+O(δ) 的误差界；同时在小 δ 区间中证明该界是紧的，并由认证过程显式保证该区间存在。

Conclusion: 结构化认证能够替代粗粒度的全局保证，为通用智能体的可部署性提供局部、可解释且可证明的依据，尤其适用于识别长时程规划中哪些具体转移是可靠的。

Abstract: In the big-world regime, agents cannot be universally capable and their ability is inevitably specialized across a world model in pieces. Consequently, standard uniform guarantees fail to distinguish between the understanding of critical bottlenecks and irrelevant failures. We first formalize this limitation by proving that general agents are not universal, rendering standard worst-case analysis uninformative. To overcome this, we introduce structural certification, a transition-local framework that maps bounded goal-conditioned performance to entry-wise guarantees on the agent's internal world model. Our main contribution is constructive. We provide algorithms that filter specific transitions using deep compositional goals and prove that a general agent on these goals has a structural world model with a $\mathcal{O}(1/n) + \mathcal{O}(δ)$ error bound. Conversely, this bound is tight in the small-$δ$ regime, whose existence is explicitly guaranteed by our certification. These results enable the certifiable deployment of general agents by localizing the specific transitions where long-horizon planning is reliable.

</details>


### [254] [OpenThoughts-Agent: Data Recipes for Agentic Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24855)
*Negin Raoof,Richard Zhuang,Marianna Nezhurina,Etash Guha,Atula Tejaswi,Ryan Marten,Charlie F. Ruan,Tyler Griggs,Alexander Glenn Shaw,Hritik Bansal,E. Kelly Buchanan,Artem Gazizov,Reinhard Heckel,Chinmay Hegde,Sankalp Jajee,Daanish Khazi,Emmanouil Koukoumidis,Xiangyi Li,Hange Liu,Shlok Natarajan,Harsh Raj,Nicholas Roberts,Ethan Shen,Nishad Singhi,Michael Siu,Ashima Suvarna,Hanwen Xing,Patrick Yubeaton,Robert Zhang,Leon Liangyu Chen,Xiaokun Chen,Steven Dillmann,Saadia Gabriel,Xunyi Jiang,Anurag Kashyap,Boxuan Li,Yein Park,Minh Pham,Sujay Sanghavi,Lin Shi,Ke Sun,Yixin Wang,Zhiwei Xu,Erica Zhang,Siyan Zhao,Wanjia Zhao,Jenia Jitsev,Alex Dimakis,Benjamin Feuer,Ludwig Schmidt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OpenThoughts-Agent 提出并开源了一个面向通用智能体模型训练的数据构建流水线，通过系统消融实验与 100K 训练样本微调，在多个智能体基准上优于现有开源数据训练模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体语言模型应用范围迅速扩大，但关于如何为具备广泛能力的智能体模型筛选和构建训练数据，公开研究仍不足。已有开源工作多针对单一基准或任务，难以回答如何训练能跨多样化智能体任务泛化的模型。

Method: 作者提出 OpenThoughts-Agent 数据构建流水线，并进行超过 100 次受控消融实验，系统分析流水线各阶段，尤其是任务来源与任务多样性的重要性。随后从该流水线构建 100K 训练样本，用于微调 Qwen3-32B，并在七个智能体基准上进行评估，同时进行计算量受控的数据规模对比实验。

Result: 微调后的模型在七个智能体基准上的平均准确率达到 44.8%，相比最强现有开源数据智能体模型 Nemotron-Terminal-32B 的 40.9% 提升 3.9 个百分点。该训练数据在不同训练集规模下均优于其他开源数据集，表现出良好的 scaling properties。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，系统化、开放的数据筛选与构建流程能够显著提升智能体模型的跨任务泛化能力；任务来源和多样性是关键因素。作者开源数据集、流水线、实验数据和模型，为后续智能体模型训练研究提供了可复现基础。

Abstract: Agentic language models dramatically expand the applications of AI yet little is publicly known about how to curate training data for broadly capable agents. Existing open efforts such as SWE-Smith, SERA, and Nemotron-Terminal typically target a single benchmark, leaving open the question of how to train models that generalize across diverse agentic tasks. The OpenThoughts-Agent (OT-Agent) project addresses this gap with a fully open data curation pipeline for training agentic models. We conduct more than 100 controlled ablation experiments to systematically investigate each stage of the pipeline, yielding insights on the importance of task sources and diversity. We then assemble a training set of 100K examples from our pipeline and fine-tune Qwen3-32B on this dataset, which yields an average accuracy of 44.8% across seven agentic benchmarks and a 3.9 percentage point improvement over the strongest existing open data agentic model (Nemotron-Terminal-32B, 40.9%). Moreover, our training data exhibits strong scaling properties, outperforming alternative open datasets at every training set size in compute-controlled comparisons. We publicly release our training sets, data pipeline, experimental data, and models at openthoughts.ai to support future open research on agentic model training.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [255] [Low-Complexity Hybrid Precoding for Cell-Free Massive MU-MIMO ISAC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23709)
*Jun Zhu,Yin Xu,Aimin Tang,Ruomeng Wang,Dazhi He,Yunfeng Guan,Wenjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向无蜂窝大规模MU-MIMO ISAC系统的低复杂度混合预编码框架，在保证多目标感知精度的同时降低前传开销与计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集中式协调分布式AP会带来较高前传开销和基带计算复杂度；同时ISAC系统需要兼顾高通信速率与高精度多目标感知，因此需要一种低复杂度且性能可靠的预编码设计。

Method: 在AP端采用部分连接混合架构，将高维信道压缩为低维有效信道，并在该域进行数字预编码；将联合混合预编码建模为带PEB约束的遍历和速率最大化问题；采用交替优化求解，将PEB约束转化为可处理凸约束，并在常模流形上优化模拟预编码；针对动态用户拓扑，提出多分支RS MMSE-THP更新算法以快速刷新公共和私有数字预编码。

Result: 仿真表明，该方案在保持较高遍历和速率和准确多目标感知性能的同时，相比传统基线方法将计算复杂度降低87.02%。

Conclusion: 所提混合预编码与快速更新机制能够有效支撑CF massive MU-MIMO ISAC系统中的低复杂度高性能通信与感知协同设计。

Abstract: Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) in cell-free (CF) massive multi-user multiple-input multiple-output (MU-MIMO) system is a promising architecture for high-rate communications and high-accuracy multi-target sensing. However, centralized coordination among distributed access points (APs) incurs substantial fronthaul overhead and computation complexity. This paper proposes a low-complexity hybrid precoding framework for CF massive MU-MIMO ISAC systems with partially-connected architectures at the APs. By applying hybrid architecture at the APs, the proposed framework converts the original high-dimensional channel information into a low-dimensional effective channel, enabling digital precoding over the compressed channel domain and thereby substantially reducing both fronthaul overhead and baseband computational complexity. We formulate the joint hybrid precoding design as an ergodic sum-rate (ESR) maximization problem with position error bound (PEB) constraints to ensure multi-target sensing accuracy. An efficient alternating optimization (AO)-based solver is then developed, where the PEB constraint is reformulated into tractable convex constraints, while the digital-domain optimization is carried out over the reduced-dimensional effective channel and the analog precoding is refined on the constant-modulus manifold. For dynamic user topology, we further propose multi-branch (MB) rate-splitting (RS) minimum mean-square-error Tomlinson-Harashima precoding (MMSE-THP) update algorithm that combines multi-branch ordering with recursive MMSE-THP matrix updates, enabling common and private digital precodings to be refreshed without repeated full matrix recomputation. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed scheme achieves high ESR and accurate multi-target sensing while reducing computational complexity by 87.02\% compared with conventional baselines.

</details>


### [256] [Beyond Mutual Information: Extension Profiles and Shape Functions of Random Variable Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23849)
*Rostislav Matveev,Andrei Romashchenko*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出并研究有限值随机变量对的“扩展剖面”，用以刻画联合分布中超越边缘熵与互信息的结构信息，并通过形状函数、信息论界与图谱性质给出其边界描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统熵向量如 H(X)、H(Y)、I(X:Y) 难以完全刻画联合分布的内部结构。作者希望通过引入辅助变量 W 后形成的条件熵与条件互信息三元组，捕捉更细粒度的可扩展结构，并连接非 Shannon 型信息不等式、Gács--Körner 公共信息与谱图理论。

Method: 定义扩展剖面为所有 (H(X|W), H(Y|W), I(X:Y|W)) 的集合；引入与剖面边界非平凡部分相关的形状函数，即 Legendre--Fenchel 变换；建立其一般上下界。对于在支撑集上均匀分布的变量对，将支撑解释为双正则二部图，并利用图的组合性质与第二大特征值推导形状函数界。

Result: 论文得到形状函数的一般信息论上下界；在均匀支撑情形下，将扩展剖面与二部图的谱性质联系起来，证明第二大特征值较小的支撑图会显著限制可能的扩展类别。

Conclusion: 扩展剖面为研究联合分布的信息结构提供了新工具，揭示了互信息表示、公共信息、非 Shannon 型约束以及谱图理论之间的联系，并可用于分析互信息的结构与表示问题。

Abstract: We study the extension profile of a pair of jointly distributed finite-valued random variables $(X,Y)$, defined as the set of all triples of numbers $ (H(X|W), H(Y|W), I(X:Y|W)) $ obtained by extending the pair with an auxiliary random variable $W$. This object captures structural properties of joint distributions that are not determined solely by the entropies of $X$ and $Y$ and their mutual information.
  To describe the boundary of the extension profile, we introduce the associated shape function, defined as the Legendre--Fenchel transform of the nontrivial part of the profile boundary. We establish general upper and lower bounds on the shape function in terms of classical information-theoretic quantities.
  For pairs that are uniform on their support, we interpret the support as a biregular bipartite graph and relate the extension profile to combinatorial and spectral properties of this graph. In this setting, we derive bounds on the shape function in terms of the second-largest eigenvalue of the graph. Thus, pairs whose support graphs have a small second eigenvalue admit only a restricted class of extensions.
  Our results provide a new perspective on the information-theoretic structure of joint distributions and highlight connections among non-Shannon-type information inequalities, the Gács--Körner common information, and spectral graph theory.
  We discuss several applications of the developed framework to problems concerning the structure and representation of mutual information.

</details>


### [257] [How Many RF Chains Does a Microwave Linear Analog Computer (MiLAC) Need to Match the Fully-Digital Cramér-Rao Bound?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23986)
*Yuchen Zhang,Yu Ge,Bruno Clerckx,Tareq Y. Al-Naffouri*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文分析可调微波线性模拟计算机（MiLAC）作为天线阵列接收前端时的到达角估计性能，证明其在满足特定行空间条件时可达到全数字接收机的Cramér--Rao界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明无损互易MiLAC可用于无线通信中的灵活或容量最优波束成形，但其在感知任务，尤其是多目标到达角估计中的理论性能尚未充分研究。

Method: 针对K个远场目标，建立接收侧无损互易MiLAC合并器模型，分析Fisher信息矩阵与合并器行空间正交投影之间的关系，并通过子空间包含条件、维度计数和可调组件数量下界来刻画达到全数字性能所需条件。

Result: Fisher信息矩阵仅依赖于合并器行空间的正交投影，且不超过全数字接收机；当行空间包含2K维联合 steering--derivative 子空间时可实现零性能差距，即每个目标需要两个RF链。stem-connected MiLAC可渐近达到组件数量下界，并仅有与天线数无关的加性开销。

Conclusion: MiLAC在感知前端设计中具有理论优势：相比相同RF链数的相移器前端，它能够精确达到全数字Cramér--Rao界，并以随天线数和目标数线性增长的硬件复杂度实现高效DOA估计。

Abstract: A microwave linear analog computer (MiLAC) is a tunable microwave network that performs linear operations directly on radio-frequency signals through wave propagation. Used as an antenna-array front end, it can map many antenna signals to a small number of active RF chains. While lossless reciprocal MiLACs have been shown to provide flexible or capacity-achieving beamforming for wireless communications, their sensing performance remains largely unexplored. We analyze direction-of-arrival estimation for $K$ far-field targets using a tunable receive-side lossless reciprocal MiLAC combiner. We show that the Fisher information matrix depends on the combiner only through the orthogonal projector onto its row space and never exceeds that of a fully digital receiver. Equality holds when the row space contains the $2K$-dimensional joint steering--derivative subspace, establishing a zero-gap threshold of two RF chains per target. A dimension-counting argument lower-bounds the number of tunable components required to achieve the digital Cramér--Rao bound for every target configuration. The stem-connected MiLAC attains this bound asymptotically, up to an antenna-count-independent additive overhead, while scaling linearly with the antenna and target counts. Unlike a phase-shifter front end with the same number of RF chains, MiLAC can exactly attain the fully digital bound. Numerical results validate the analysis.

</details>


### [258] [Minimal additive codes and additive strong blocking sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24262)
*Gianira N. Alfarano,Marine Le Meur*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文引入并研究有限域扩域上的极小加性码，建立其与加性强阻塞集之间的几何对应关系，并给出构造与长度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加性码推广了线性码：它们在字母表扩域上不要求完全线性，但保持对子域的线性。已有极小线性码与有限几何对象之间有成熟联系，本文希望将类似几何框架扩展到极小加性码，以便系统刻画、构造并估计其参数。

Method: 作者定义“加性强阻塞集”，即一类 h-射影系统，其并集构成强阻塞集；随后从有限几何角度证明非退化极小加性码的等价类与加性强阻塞集的等价类之间存在一一对应。同时将该框架与外强阻塞集理论比较，说明后者是本文理论的特例。

Result: 论文建立了极小加性码与加性强阻塞集的精确对应关系，统一并推广了外强阻塞集相关理论；此外给出若干极小加性码的构造与存在性结果，并推导其最小长度的上界、下界和渐近界。

Conclusion: 该工作为极小加性码提供了新的有限几何解释，扩展了极小码理论的适用范围，并为后续研究其构造、分类与参数最优化奠定了基础。

Abstract: Additive codes over $\mathbb{F}_{q^h}$ generalize linear codes by relaxing linearity over the alphabet while retaining linearity over the subfield $\mathbb{F}_q$. In this paper, we introduce minimal additive codes and we initiate their study from a geometric perspective. We define the concept of additive strong blocking sets, a class of $h$-projective systems whose union forms a strong blocking set. We establish a one-to-one correspondence between equivalence classes of nondegenerate minimal additive codes and equivalence classes of additive strong blocking sets. We also compare this framework with the theory of outer strong blocking sets, showing that the latter arises as a special case. Finally, we provide constructions and existence results for minimal additive codes, and derive upper, lower, and asymptotic bounds on their minimum length.

</details>


### [259] [Discrepancy for Random Linear Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24471)
*Dean Doron,Tal Leonov,Jonathan Mosheiff,Henrique Navas,Nicolas Resch,João Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文证明随机线性码在多种参数范围内具有接近最优的 discrepancy 性质，并由此推出其在超容量错误列表译码、零错误列表恢复及抗局部泄露线性 ramp 秘密共享中的性能可匹配非结构化随机码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机线性码具有代数结构、实现与分析价值，但其是否能在细粒度分布均匀性、列表译码和列表恢复等任务中达到非结构化随机码的性能并不显然。作者希望建立统一的 discrepancy 定理，解释并推广随机线性码在这些场景中的近最优表现。

Method: 论文建立两个一般性定理：一个控制固定测试集合的所有平移，另一个控制大规模 Fourier 伪随机测试族。证明技术基于精细化的二阶矩分析，在逐步向随机线性码加入随机生成元时跟踪交集大小的变化。

Result: 结果表明：任意有限域上，随机线性码在高于容量的错误列表译码中，其与所有给定半径 Hamming 球的交集大小同时集中在期望值附近；在素域且适度增长的域大小下，随机线性码在高于容量的零错误列表恢复中，对所有固定坐标大小的矩形集合也具有同样的集中性。进一步推出存在丰富的抗平衡局部泄露线性 ramp 秘密共享方案，并显著降低既有阈值要求。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了随机线性码 discrepancy 行为的统一理论，说明其在若干核心编码与密码学应用中可达到与无结构随机码相当的性能，同时扩展了经典覆盖半径结果并改进了相关秘密共享方案的存在性界限。

Abstract: We prove that random linear codes have nearly optimal discrepancy properties in a broad range of regimes. Our main results are two general theorems: one controlling all translates of a fixed test, and another controlling large families of Fourier-pseudorandom tests. Two motivating applications follow.
  First, random linear codes match unstructured random codes for list-decoding from errors above capacity. If $C\subseteq\mathbb F_q^n$ is a random linear code of rate $1-\frac1n\log_q |B_ρ|+ε$, where $B_ρ$ is a radius-$ρ$ Hamming ball, then with high probability $$ |C\cap B|=(1\pm o(1))\frac{|C||B|}{q^n} $$ simultaneously for all radius-$ρ$ Hamming balls $B\subseteq\mathbb F_q^n$. This extends the classical result that such codes have covering radius at most $ρn$ whp (Blinovsky, 1987).
  Second, over prime fields, random linear codes match unstructured random codes for zero-error list-recovery above capacity. For prime $q>2$ and $2\le \ell\le q-1$, a random linear code of rate $1-\log_q\ell+ε$ satisfies, with high probability, $$ |C\cap S|=(1\pm o(1))\frac{|C|\ell^n}{q^n} $$ simultaneously for all rectangles $S=S_1\times\cdots\times S_n$ with $|S_i|=\ell$. As a consequence, there are abundant $n$-party linear ramp secret sharing schemes over $\mathbb F_q$ with privacy threshold about $n/(2\log q)$ and reconstruction threshold about $5n/(2\log q)$, resilient to balanced local leakage; prior existence results required thresholds above $n/2$ even in this case.
  The translate result, hence the list-decoding application, holds over arbitrary finite fields, even growing with $n$. The list-recovery and leakage applications hold over prime fields under moderate growth, e.g. $q\le n^{1/5-o(1)}$. The proofs use a refined second-moment analysis tracking intersection sizes as random generators are added to $C$.

</details>


### [260] [Importance Sampling for Event Discovery via Guesswork](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24537)
*Asaf Cohen*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文将重要性采样从稀有事件概率估计转向快速发现稀有轨迹，提出以名义模型下发现轨迹的描述长度/惊讶度为核心的发现质量准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统重要性采样主要通过降低估计器方差来高效估计稀有事件概率，但许多实际任务更关心尽快生成一个落入稀有集合的轨迹，而非精确估计概率。因此需要以命中时间和发现轨迹质量为目标的新设计原则。

Method: 作者形式化定义Quality of Discovery问题，将目标设为最小化被发现轨迹在名义分布下的描述长度，并证明其等价于最小化名义排序指数/guesswork指数。针对独立同分布模型和由类型定义的稀有集合，比较经典IS目标与发现最优目标。

Result: 经典IS选择最小化D(Q||p)的质量主导类型，而发现最优选择最小化H(Q)+D(Q||p)的类型。该目标刻画了在名义模型搜索顺序下稀有集合中“最不令人惊讶”的代表序列；在预算约束下，当命中时间最优解不唯一时，该指数可作为字典序式的决策准则。

Conclusion: 论文建立了发现导向重要性采样的理论框架，指出H(Q)+D(Q||p)是自然目标函数，并在随机采样与系统化搜索之间建立形式化联系。

Abstract: Traditional importance sampling (IS) is designed to estimate rare-event probabilities by minimizing estimator variance. However, many applications prioritize rapid discovery: the generation of a trajectory within a rare set $A_n$. This requires a shift from ensemble-based estimation to a design principle focused on the hitting time $τ_{A_n} := \inf\{t \ge 1 : Y_t^n \in A_n\}$.
  We formalize a Quality of Discovery problem as the problem of minimizing the description length (surprisal) of the discovered trajectory under the nominal model $p$. We prove that minimizing this description length is equivalent to minimizing the nominal rank exponent $J_{\mathrm{rank}}(q_n) := \lim_{n\to\infty} \frac{1}{n} \log G_n(Y^n)$, where $G_n(x^n)$ is the guesswork of sequence $x^n$. For i.i.d.\ models and type-defined rare sets $Γ$, we show that while classical IS targets the mass-dominating type $Q_{\mathrm{IS}}^* \in \arg\min_{Q \in Γ} D(Q\|p)$, discovery optimality is achieved by $Q_{\mathrm{GW}}^* \in \arg\min_{Q \in Γ} [H(Q) + D(Q\|p)]$. This framework identifies a fundamental rule: minimizing the guesswork exponent ensures the discovered sequence is the "least surprising" representative of the set relative to the nominal model's search order. We further demonstrate that under budgetary constraints, this exponent serves as a lexicographic tie-breaker when the hitting-time minimizer is not unique. This establishes $H(Q) + D(Q\|p)$ as a natural objective for discovery-based importance sampling, providing a formal bridge between randomized sampling and systematic search.

</details>


### [261] [Time-varying Wireless Channel Tracking with Online Parameter Learning via the Birth-Death-Drift Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24727)
*Tiancheng Gao,Mohamed Akrout,Faouzi Bellili,Amine Mezghani*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出BDD-VAMP-EM，用于在动态传播环境中以低导频开销获取大规模MIMO的高精度CSI；该方法结合BDD时序模型、VAMP与EM，自动估计模型参数并提升实际适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有利用时间相关性降低导频开销的方法往往依赖不现实假设。AMP-SI虽利用BDD模型取得进展，但存在三类部署障碍：要求独立同分布高斯感知矩阵、需要已知精确BDD参数、对时序信息处理仅为统计近似。

Method: 作者构建统一的BDD-VAMP-EM框架：用BDD模型刻画信道支撑的出生、死亡和漂移动态；用VAMP替代传统AMP以适配更一般的感知矩阵；用EM自动学习未知BDD/统计参数，从而减少人工调参和先验依赖。

Result: 仿真结果显示，BDD-VAMP-EM在多种设置下稳定优于现有基准方法，尤其在模型参数不匹配时优势更明显。

Conclusion: BDD-VAMP-EM缓解了AMP-SI在矩阵假设、参数依赖和时序信息利用方面的局限，具备更强鲁棒性和更高实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Accurate massive MIMO channel state information (CSI) acquisition with low pilot overhead is critical in dynamic propagation environments. Exploiting temporal correlation is key to reducing pilot overhead, yet most existing methods often rely on impractical assumptions. The approximate message passing with side information (AMP-SI) algorithm, built upon a birth-death-drift (BDD) model, represents a significant step in this direction. However, its practical deployment is hindered by three major limitations: reliance on i.i.d. Gaussian sensing matrices, need for perfect BDD parameter knowledge, and a statistically approximate treatment of temporal information. To address these limitations, we introduce BDD-VAMP-EM, a fully automated algorithm that relies on the BDD model, vector AMP (VAMP), and expectation-maximization (EM) in a unified framework. Simulations show that BDD-VAMP-EM consistently outperforms existing benchmarks, particularly under model parameter mismatch, confirming its practical viability.

</details>
